Classifier now returns a `candidates: [{intakeType, score, margin, hits}]` top-3 array so reviewers see alternatives without re-running. Regression coverage in `tests/intake-classifier-calibration.test.mjs` + corpus at `tests/fixtures/intake/golden-rnd.json`. Backward compatible: the locked golden test (`tests/intake/golden-rnd.test.mjs`) still passes byte-for-byte.

### Added

- **Universal `{ text, structured, droppedInfo }` extractor contract across all intake formats.** Every path through `extractDocumentText` now returns a `droppedInfo: []` array of `{ kind, count, reason, recoverable }` objects describing content that was silently dropped during extraction. New `lib/extractors/shared/drop-info.mjs` provides the factory. VTT and SRT files now produce `structured: { format, cues, speakers }` via a pure-JS parser in `lib/extractors/transcript.mjs` that handles voice spans (`<v Name>`), cue identifiers, timing, and settings without external dependencies. ICS calendar files produce `structured: { events: [...] }` via `lib/extractors/calendar.mjs` which unfolds RFC 5545 continuation lines and extracts SUMMARY, DTSTART, DTEND, ORGANIZER, ATTENDEE, DESCRIPTION, LOCATION, and RRULE. DOCX extraction now reports table count, embedded image count, and review comment count in `droppedInfo`. XLSX reports formula cell count and embedded images. PPTX reports speaker notes slide count, slide animation count, and embedded images. EML extraction reports dropped HTML body parts and inline images. PDFs below a per-page text threshold emit `kind: "scanned-pdf"` with a pointer to `docs/intake/scanned-pdfs.md`. Audio and video files (`.mp3`, `.wav`, `.m4a`, `.mp4`, `.mov`, and 6 other formats) are now in `EXTRACTABLE_DOCUMENT_EXTS` so the inbox scanner picks them up, but `extractDocumentText` throws a typed `{ code: "ASR_REQUIRED" }` error instead of a generic "unsupported type" error; `InboxWatcher.poll()` catches this and routes the file to `~/.cx/intake/needs-asr/<id>.json` with the original signal preserved. Two new CLI commands: `construct intake extraction-warnings [--since=7d] [--kind=table] [--limit=50]` summarizes the JSONL warning log; `construct intake needs-asr list|show <id>` shows the ASR-pending queue. Documentation: `docs/intake/audio-video.md` and `docs/intake/scanned-pdfs.md`.

- **Quarantine queue for low-confidence intake packets.** New `.cx/intake/quarantine/` directory holds packets where `confidence < 0.60` OR `margin < 0.20`, so uncertainty never auto-routes to a specialist who would act on a confident-looking-but-wrong label. `lib/intake/quarantine.mjs` exports `shouldQuarantine(triage)` (the decision), `writeQuarantinePacket`, `listQuarantine`, `readQuarantine`, and `rerouteQuarantined`. Both `FilesystemIntakeQueue.enqueue` (solo) and `PostgresIntakeQueue.enqueue` (team/enterprise) call `shouldQuarantine` and route accordingly; the Postgres path uses a new `status='quarantined'` value on the existing `construct_intake_items.status` column (no schema migration needed). Three new CLI subcommands: `construct intake quarantine list`, `construct intake quarantine show <id>` (renders top-3 candidates with margins), and `construct intake reroute <id> --type=<intakeType> [--reason="..."]` which promotes to pending AND writes a learned fixture for CI regression coverage.
- **Override-becomes-fixture loop closes the feedback gap.** `construct intake reroute` writes a JSON record to `tests/fixtures/intake/learned/<content-hash>.json` whenever a human reclassifies a quarantined packet. CI loads every learned fixture via `tests/intake-classifier-calibration.test.mjs` and asserts the classifier handles each case correctly — the same wrong call cannot ship twice without also failing the build. Daemon determinism preserved: fixtures are loaded only by tests, never consulted at runtime. New helper `fixtureHash(sourcePath, extractedText)` keys fixtures by content sha256 so duplicate signals produce one fixture, not many.
- **Expected Calibration Error as a CI gate.** `tests/intake-classifier-calibration.test.mjs` computes ECE across the golden + learned corpus using 10-bin equal-width binning. Hard assertions: high-confidence predictions (≥0.70) are always correct (the trust-loss failure mode); low-confidence predictions (`<0.60` and not `unknown`) always route to quarantine; ECE on the high-confidence subset stays ≤0.15. Threshold tightens as the learned-fixture corpus diversifies — current floor reflects an all-correct small corpus where ECE is bounded by `(1 - mean_confidence)`.

- **Tag vocabulary and lifecycle system.** New `config/tag-vocabulary.json` defines the repo-wide controlled vocabulary: five orthogonal facets (`intake-type`, `lifecycle`, `priority`, `signal-strength`, `reversibility`), per-facet exclusivity and cardinality budgets, tag lifecycle states (`active`, `deprecated`, `archived`), and four tags that promote to graph entities (`signal/high-customer-interest`, `priority/p0`, `priority/p1`, `reversibility/irreversible`). Project-local overrides live at `.cx/tags/vocabulary-overrides.json` (no PR required). `lib/tags/vocabulary.mjs` loads and merges both tiers. Tag CLI subcommands: `construct tags propose`, `add`, `promote-to-repo`, `deprecate`, `archive`, `merge`, `split`, `rollback-migration`, and `audit`. `construct tags audit` reports active vs. over-budget tags, deprecated tags still in use, unknown tags, and orphans. New Postgres migration `db/schema/007_tags.sql` adds `construct_tag_attribution` (per-document source + confidence history) and `construct_tag_migrations` (merge/split audit trail) tables, plus a GIN index on `construct_documents.tags`. `construct tags backfill` populates from frontmatter on disk.

- **Auto-tag attribution via intake classifier.** `lib/intake/classify.mjs` exports `suggestTags(triage, related, vocab)` — a pure, synchronous function that maps the winning `intakeType` to an `intake/<type>` tag (at triage confidence) and inherits tags held by 2 or more related documents (at 0.70 confidence). Attribution records carry `{ tag, source: 'agent:classifier' | 'agent:related-inherit', confidence }`. Tags below the per-facet `auto_threshold` are held in `triage.suggestedTags` for human pick-up via `construct intake done`; tags above threshold are stamped into artifact frontmatter by `lib/doc-stamp.mjs`. Deprecated and archived tags are filtered from suggestions when a vocab is supplied. Daemon path remains deterministic (no LLM calls).

- **Tag-aware knowledge search.** `knowledgeSearch` in `lib/knowledge/search.mjs` accepts `tags: string[]` and `tagMatch: 'any' | 'all'` options. Chunks are filtered before scoring, so tag pre-filtering composes cleanly with BM25 + cosine hybrid ranking. New `lib/knowledge/postgres-search.mjs` provides a selectivity-aware SQL path: when the tagged fraction of the corpus is below 5%, a GIN prefilter runs before the HNSW vector scan; otherwise HNSW runs with `hnsw.iterative_scan = relaxed_order` and tag filtering applies post-scan. The `knowledge_graph_ask` MCP tool gains a `tags` parameter so graph traversals can be tag-restricted from the MCP client side.

- **Per-skill usage observability.** `logSkillCall` in `lib/telemetry/skill-calls.mjs` now captures four optional extended fields: `latencyMs` (skill-load wall time), `agentId` (calling specialist identity), `sessionId` (active session id for quality correlation), and `tokensReturned` (loaded skill size for cost attribution). All fields are backward-compatible with earlier log files. `summarizeSkillCalls` adds `since`, `agentId`, and `source` filters; summary slots now include `agents[]`, `sessions` count, and `p50LatencyMs`/`p95LatencyMs` percentiles. New exports: `findOrphanSkills({ logPath, since, allSkillIds })` (pruning candidates), `hotSkills({ logPath, since, limit })` (top-K by call count). New CLI subcommands: `construct skills usage [--since=30d] [--agent=X] [--source=mcp]`, `construct skills orphans`, `construct skills hot`, and `construct skills correlate-quality`. New Postgres migration `db/schema/008_skill_usage.sql` adds `construct_skill_invocations` table with indexes and a `construct_skill_quality_correlation` view joining against `construct_cx_scores`. `lib/role-preload.mjs` and `lib/mcp/tools/skills.mjs` both pass latency and caller metadata to `logSkillCall`.

- **OTel GenAI semantic conventions and W3C traceparent propagation.** New `lib/telemetry/otel-tracer.mjs` provides a graceful-degrade OTel facade: when `@opentelemetry/api` is installed and `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT` is set, real SDK spans are emitted with `gen_ai.*` attributes; otherwise a no-op tracer runs with zero overhead and no network attempts. `GenAiAttrs` constants cover `gen_ai.system`, `gen_ai.operation.name`, `gen_ai.request.model`, `gen_ai.response.model`, `gen_ai.usage.input_tokens`, `gen_ai.usage.output_tokens`, `gen_ai.response.finish_reasons`, `gen_ai.tool.name`, and `gen_ai.tool.call.id`. `withGenAiSpan(operationName, attrs, fn)` wraps an async callback, records errors, and closes the span on completion. W3C trace context propagates across MCP boundaries via `injectTraceContext(meta)` / `extractTraceContext(meta)` which read and write `traceparent`/`tracestate` into `params._meta` per SEP-414. `lib/distill.mjs` and `lib/hooks/mcp-audit.mjs` are instrumented. The `@opentelemetry/*` packages are listed in `optionalDependencies` so installs without an OTel backend are unaffected. New `docs/concepts/observability.mdx` documents local-only mode, Langfuse, Honeycomb, Grafana Tempo, and Datadog AI Observability integrations.

- **Doc hygiene scanner and `stampVerified` utility.** New `lib/hygiene/scan.mjs` exports `findHygieneCandidates({ cwd, scopes, limit })` which returns the oldest N (default 25) documents that need verification: `lifecycle/approved` docs with `last_verified_at` older than 30 days, `lifecycle/draft` docs older than 7 days, and any doc missing the field. Tags in inline YAML bracket notation (`[lifecycle/approved]`) are parsed correctly. `stampVerified(filePath, { date })` writes or updates `last_verified_at` in YAML frontmatter (prepending a frontmatter block when none exists). The `doc-hygiene-scan` scheduler job runs `findHygieneCandidates` and returns `{ status: 'ok', candidates: N }`.

- **Scheduler abstraction (`lib/scheduler/index.mjs`).** Unified background-job registry supporting `registerJob`, `runJobOnce`, and `listJobs`. Three built-in jobs pre-registered: `tag-candidate-mining` (daily, mines unknown-tag clusters), `skill-usage-rollup` (hourly, rolls up skill-call JSONL), and `doc-hygiene-scan` (hourly, surfaces stale lifecycle docs). CLI: `construct scheduler list`, `construct scheduler run <id>`. In solo mode jobs write native triggers (launchctl/systemd); in team/enterprise mode jobs coordinate via Postgres advisory locks.

- **SKILL.md directory format and dual-target sync.** Each top-level skill is now a directory containing `SKILL.md` with YAML frontmatter (`name`, `description`, `allowed-tools`). `scripts/sync-specialists.mjs` writes to both `.claude/skills/<name>/SKILL.md` (Claude Code) and `.agents/skills/<name>/SKILL.md` (Codex) from the `skills/` source of truth. Loader falls back to the flat `<name>.md` form for one minor release. `scripts/migrate-skill-frontmatter.mjs` is the one-shot migration that converts HTML-comment preamble to YAML frontmatter (idempotent, dry-run default). New `docs/adr/` entry records the skills-on-disk-layout decision.

- **Workflow templatization.** New `templates/workflows/` directory with three instantiable YAML templates: `new-feature.yml` (PRD + ADR + test-plan artifacts with linked beads items), `engineering-onboarding.yml` (day-1/day-3/week-1 checkpoints), and `cross-team-handoff.yml` (handoff brief + owner mapping + success criteria). Each template carries `inputs`, `artifacts`, `beads_items`, and `checks` sections. CLI: `construct workflow list`, `construct workflow new <id> [--name=X] [--non-interactive]`, `construct workflow show <id>`. `lib/workflows/instantiate.mjs` renders templates against inputs and creates artifacts + beads items with dependency order. `schemas/workflow.schema.json` validates all templates. New cookbook pages: `docs/cookbook/onboard-a-new-engineer.mdx`, `docs/cookbook/track-research-findings.mdx`, `docs/cookbook/cross-team-handoff.mdx`. `docs/concepts/profile-inheritance.mdx` documents `extends:` resolution order with a worked example.

- **Provider allowlist scope framing and `construct creds` CLI.** Provider configurations now carry per-resource allowlists: `repoAllowlist`/`repoAllowGlob` for GitHub, `projectAllowlist` for Jira, `spaceAllowlist` for Confluence, `channelAllowlist` for Slack. Requests targeting resources outside the allowlist return a typed `OUT_OF_SCOPE` error with the allowlist contents for debugging. `lib/providers/contract.mjs` defines the allowlist schema. `lib/providers/circuit-breaker.mjs` gains `getState(name)` and `reset(name)` methods. CLI: `construct creds set|rotate|list|revoke|test <provider>` manages credentials in `~/.construct/config.env` (chmod 0600 enforced); `construct providers status [--reset=<name>]` shows per-provider health and breaker state; `construct providers discover <name>` runs provider-specific discovery (repos, projects, channels) with results cached at `.cx/providers/discovery-<name>.json`; `construct provider new <name> --capabilities=read,search` scaffolds a new provider module with pre-wired contract shape and a minimal health test.

- **`tests/fixtures/projects/agentic-hq/` fixture and end-to-end integration test.** Relocates the ephemeral `/tmp/agentic-hq/` fixture into the repo at `tests/fixtures/projects/agentic-hq/`. Contains 6 classified inbox files (postmortem, exec-ask, eval finding, legal question, perf trace, UX research), a WebVTT standup transcript with two speakers, a stale ADR (`last_verified_at: 2025-11-01`, `lifecycle/approved`), plus skeleton `docs/prd/`, `docs/rfc/`, and `docs/research/` directories. New functional test `tests/functional/agentic-hq-integration.functional.test.mjs` exercises 8 describe blocks against the fixture: intake classifier (demo regression guards: research note is not `security`; eval finding is not `bug`), `suggestTags` output shape, tag-aware knowledge search filtering, doc-hygiene scanner (stale ADR detected, `stampVerified` round-trip), scheduler job registry (`listJobs` + `runJobOnce`), skill-call telemetry extended payload, workflow template directory count, OTel no-op fallback when endpoint unset, and VTT structured extraction (2 speakers, empty `droppedInfo`).

## [1.0.7] - 2026-05-27

### Fixed

- **`ensureGitHooksPath` now rewires when `core.hooksPath` is the git default.** The setup helper previously treated any non-empty `core.hooksPath` as a user-set value and refused to wire `.beads/hooks` on top of it. But `.git/hooks` (or an absolute path to it) is the *default* git stores when an unset user accepts the implicit value: not an active choice, just the absence of one. With the old logic, an install that landed while `core.hooksPath` happened to be the default left Construct's pre-commit policy gates inactive forever, and `construct doctor` reported "Git hooks unwired" with no path to recovery short of manual `git config`. The fix recognizes both forms of the default (`.git/hooks` relative and absolute) as equivalent to unset and proceeds to wire `.beads/hooks`. A custom non-default `core.hooksPath` (e.g. `tools/git-hooks/`) is still left alone with a warning. Regression coverage in `tests/setup-git-hooks-path.test.mjs`.
- **Intake classifier no longer misroutes postmortems as bug reports and is now deterministic.** `lib/intake/classify.mjs` had three compounding problems against the real corpus: a postmortem with "crash" and "exception" in the body matched the bug type first-wins; a research note that mentioned "outage" in passing routed to incident; and the outcomes-aware tiebreaker fed cached success-rate history into classification, so the same file could land in different buckets across runs. The fix adds filename-pattern weighting (e.g. `postmortem` adds +0.4 to `incident`, `adr-*` to `architecture`, `prd-*` to `requirement`, `-bug.*` to `bug`), title-level negative keywords (`# Postmortem`, `# Incident report`, `# ADR-...` apply a −0.5 penalty to `bug` so a postmortem describing a bug in retrospect routes correctly), and removes the outcomes coupling entirely. Confidence is now capped at 0.5 when the runner-up is within 0.1 of the winner. The `cwd` argument is still accepted for backward compatibility but is not consulted. Regression coverage in `tests/intake-classifier-accuracy.test.mjs`.
- **`.eml` files no longer silently skipped during intake extraction.** `lib/document-extract.mjs` did not recognize the email extension, which dropped customer-email-as-signal flows on the floor without warning. Added a minimal RFC 5322 parser that unfolds headers, walks multipart boundaries, decodes quoted-printable and base64 transfer encodings, and concatenates `Subject`, `From`, `Date`, and the first `text/plain` part into the text the classifier reads. Attachments are excluded from the text and exposed on the result's `attachments` array. Messages above the 50 MB hard cap return empty text plus `skipped: 'too large'` rather than throwing. No new runtime dependency. Regression coverage in `tests/intake-eml-extraction.test.mjs`.
- **`construct embed start` now resolves rootDir from the project, not just homedir.** Previously, `resolveRootDir` returned `env.CX_DATA_DIR || homedir()`, so starting the daemon inside a Construct-initialized project watched `~/.cx/inbox/` instead of `<project>/.cx/inbox/`; the only workaround (`CX_DATA_DIR=$PWD`) was undiscoverable. The new precedence is (1) `CX_DATA_DIR` if explicitly set, (2) walk up from `process.cwd()` looking for `.cx/context.md` (capped at 10 parent levels), (3) `homedir()` fallback. `construct embed status` now prints the resolved `rootDir` and the inbox path it is watching alongside other status info. Regression tests in `tests/embed-rootdir-resolution.test.mjs`.
- **Profile `rebrand` config now drives user-facing intake terminology.** Each profile JSON carries `rebrand: { intakeQueueLabel, signalNoun }`, but the CLI hardcoded "intake queue" / "signal" / "intake" everywhere regardless of active profile, so `operations` projects still saw "intake" instead of "request" in every surface. A new `lib/profiles/rebrand.mjs` exports `getRebrand(rootDir)` with safe defaults (`Intake queue`, `signal`). `construct intake list` / `show` / `--help`, the embed daemon's classification log line, and the session-start intake prelude all route through the helper. Regression tests in `tests/profile-rebrand.test.mjs`.
- **Test suite no longer leaks `/tmp/construct-*` directories on every run.** Roughly 6,400 `/tmp` dirs had accumulated across at least 15 prefix families (`construct-sync-repo`, `construct-mcp-home`, `construct-github-cwd`, `construct-atlassian-*`, `construct-plugin-mcp-*`, `construct-bad-opencode-*`, `construct-opencode-plugin-*`, `construct-hybrid-env-*`, `construct-user-env-*`, `construct-remove-*`, `construct-mcp-cwd-*`, and others). Root cause: ~30 test files called `fs.mkdtempSync(...)` without a corresponding `fs.rmSync` or `t.after` cleanup, and shared helpers in `tests/helpers.mjs` and `tests/mcp-manager.test.mjs#tempDir` had no cleanup contract. Fix wires per-test cleanup into every offender (preferring `t.after(() => fs.rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }))` for per-test temp dirs, `after()` blocks with a tracked-dirs array for helper-style temp factories). The shared `tempDir(prefix, t)` helpers now accept the test context and register cleanup automatically when supplied. Regression guard `tests/test-hygiene.test.mjs` walks every `tests/**/*.mjs` file and fails the suite if a `mkdtempSync` call site lands without a sibling cleanup signal; the allowlist documents the single legitimate exemption (`helpers.mjs`, whose cleanup is delegated to the caller). Closes `construct-44p`.
- **`construct init --yes` no longer auto-registers project artifact directories as inbox watchers.** Previously, init scanned the project for known preset directories (`src`, `lib`, `packages`, `apps`, `services`, `docs`, `tests`, `spec`, `infra`, `config`, `scripts`, `tools`) and wrote every one that existed into `.cx/intake-config.json` as a `parentDir`. The intake daemon then treated every file in those directories as a fresh inbox signal: code commits, finished PRDs, ADRs, RFCs, and test files all got classified and routed as if they were new R&D signals, polluting the queue with false positives that drowned out real signals from `.cx/inbox/`. The new default is an empty `parentDirs` list. The built-in zones (`.cx/inbox/` and `docs/intake/` when it exists) remain watched. Extra dirs are opt-in via `construct intake config set --add-dir=<path>`. Init now always writes `.cx/intake-config.json` (previously only when parentDirs was non-empty) so users can inspect and edit the config. Regression test in `tests/init-intake-defaults.test.mjs`.
- **Embed daemon log no longer grows unbounded.** Two compounding bugs caused `~/.cx/runtime/embed-daemon.log` to reach 34 GB on a single local install:
  1. The scheduler in `lib/embed/scheduler.mjs` silently dropped the `repeat: false` option, so a one-shot startup job registered with `intervalMs: 0` became `setInterval(fn, 0)` and fired on every event-loop tick (~thousands per second). The job ran `runTelemetrySetup`, returned the "keys required" error, and (via stderr) wrote a log line each time. Scheduler now honors `repeat: false` and routes the task through a single `setTimeout` (or no timer at all when paired with `runImmediately: true`). Regression test in `tests/embed-scheduler.test.mjs`.
  2. The daemon log had no rotation policy. The parent process opens an append-mode FD at spawn time and the worker holds it for its full lifetime, so rotation has to happen before the spawn opens the FD. `lib/embed/cli.mjs` now calls a new `rotateEmbedLogIfNeeded(log, env)` at each spawn site (both `cmdEmbedStart` and `autoStartEmbedIfNeeded`). Cap defaults to 50 MB with 3 retained segments (worst-case 200 MB on disk). Override with `CONSTRUCT_EMBED_LOG_MAX_MB` and `CONSTRUCT_EMBED_LOG_KEEP`; both clamped to a sane hard cap. Regression tests in `tests/embed-log-rotation.test.mjs`.

### Added

- **Full typed contract coverage across the 28-specialist roster.** Closes the Bet 5 contracts gap surfaced by the audit: 13 specialists were previously reachable only via wildcard `* → cx-X` fallbacks and had no structured handoff, so dispatch into them was unauditable. `specialists/contracts.json` now defines 24 typed producer→consumer contracts for `cx-rd-lead`, `cx-ux-researcher`, `cx-operations`, `cx-business-strategist`, `cx-data-analyst`, `cx-evaluator`, `cx-platform-engineer`, `cx-legal-compliance`, `cx-accessibility`, `cx-explorer`, `cx-trace-reviewer`, `cx-data-engineer`, and `cx-test-automation`. Each contract carries triggers (e.g., `framingChallenge.required` for `cx-rd-lead`, `riskFlags: ["compliance"]` for `cx-legal-compliance`), required input/output fields, preconditions tied to role authority, and structured postconditions (`artifact-has-section`, `artifact-claims-cited`) for the artifacts each role produces — framing briefs with Problem/Constraints/Open Questions, strategic memos with Market Context/Strategic Risks, eval specs with Success Criteria/Baseline, compliance memos with Regulatory Citations/Remediation Plan, pipeline specs with Idempotency Plan/Observability, automation plans with Flake Budget. New `tests/contracts-coverage.test.mjs` asserts every specialist in `specialists/registry.json` appears as a non-wildcard producer or consumer in `specialists/contracts.json` and that every producer/consumer name resolves against the registry.
- **Self-maintenance on version upgrade.** New `lib/maintenance/cleanup.mjs` module collects the primitives that enforce artifact lifecycle: rotate oversized embed-daemon log segments, tail-truncate oversized `~/.cx/*.jsonl` event logs while preserving recent records (caps: 25 MB audit/violations/doctor/role-events, 5 MB role-pending), and age-out `~/.construct/cache/` entries older than 30 days. Orchestrator `runFullCleanup` runs under a 5-second hard time budget; individual primitive failures are non-fatal. The trigger is automatic: on every `construct` invocation, the CLI compares the installed version against `~/.construct/.cleanup-stamp`. A mismatch (or absent stamp) runs a single bounded cleanup pass before dispatching the command, then writes the new stamp. Subsequent invocations are a single file read. This is what unblocks existing installs that already carry multi-GB logs from before this release: upgrade alone reclaims the disk. `CONSTRUCT_DISABLE_AUTO_CLEANUP=1` opts out. Skipped for `construct hook <name>` invocations to keep harness-driven hook latency low.
- **`construct cleanup --disk-only` / `--dry-run` / `--pressure-only`** flags. The existing process-pressure-release command now also runs the disk maintenance sweep by default; flags carve out either half. `--dry-run` previews disk cleanup without touching files. Regression tests in `tests/maintenance-cleanup.test.mjs`.
- **Anti-fabrication contract on every specialist prompt and the construct persona.** The canonical `rules/common/no-fabrication.md` policy was previously referenced by only 6 of 28 specialist prompts (`cx-architect`, `cx-debugger`, `cx-engineer`, `cx-orchestrator`, `cx-qa`, `cx-reviewer`). The remaining 22 specialists and `personas/construct.md` itself now each carry a role-tailored Anti-fabrication contract paragraph. Each contract names the specific fabrication risk for that role (e.g., `cx-product-manager` flags fabricated quotes and uncited user-demand summaries; `cx-data-analyst` flags fabricated percentages and unsourced trend claims; `cx-legal-compliance` flags fabricated regulatory requirements) and links to the canonical policy. The persona carries an umbrella contract that specialists inherit from. Regression test `tests/no-fabrication-coverage.test.mjs` enforces (a) coverage stays at 28/28 + persona, (b) every contract links to the canonical file, and (c) no two specialist contracts are byte-identical (each must name a role-specific risk).
- **`STRATEGY.md` at the repo root.** Northstar, bets, non-bets, time horizon, phased path, and open bets for Construct itself. Sits above [`docs/prd/0001-construct-org-in-a-box.md`](docs/prd/0001-construct-org-in-a-box.md), which remains the spec layer.
- **`rules/common/review-before-change.md`** encodes a "review current state before authoring artifacts" rule. Extends the existing code-side discipline in [`development-workflow.md`](rules/common/development-workflow.md) step 0 to docs, rules, templates, strategies, PRDs, ADRs, and RFCs. The intent is to prevent duplicate sibling artifacts.
- **`ensureCxDir(rootDir)` in `lib/project-init-shared.mjs`.** Shared utility that creates `.cx/` and initializes `context.md` from the project template in a single atomic call. All code paths that previously created `.cx/` without `context.md` (`lib/hooks/mcp-audit.mjs`, `lib/intake/intake-config.mjs`, `lib/embed/inbox.mjs`) now route through this utility.
- **`rules/common/no-fabrication.md` — canonical anti-fabrication policy.** Single source of truth for trust-preserving outputs: cite load-bearing claims, surface gaps with `unknown` or `[unverified]` rather than papering them over, preserve ambiguity, distinguish observation from inference, calibrate confidence honestly, never invent quotes/ticket-IDs/percentages/dates/file-paths. Referenced from `AGENTS.md`, `CLAUDE.md`, and the six specialist prompts (`cx-architect`, `cx-debugger`, `cx-engineer`, `cx-orchestrator`, `cx-qa`, `cx-reviewer`) that did not previously carry an evidence rule.
- **Artifact-prose lint in `lib/comment-lint.mjs`.** New `BANNED_ARTIFACT` pattern bank flags manufactured confidence (`clearly/obviously/undoubtedly`), unattributed percentages, customer mind-reading (`users want / customers expect`), and speculative projections (`will likely / should improve`) in artifact paths only (`docs/prd/**`, `docs/adr/**`, `docs/rfc/**`, `docs/research/**`, `.cx/knowledge/**`, `.cx/handoffs/**`, `.cx/research/**`). Code blocks, headings, table rows, and YAML frontmatter are skipped. Severity is controlled by `CONSTRUCT_ARTIFACT_LINT_MODE`: PostToolUse hook defaults to `warn` (advisory, edit not blocked) so drafting can iterate; CLI / pre-commit / CI / release-gate set `block` so fabrication cannot land.
- **Intake traceability stamping (`lib/intake/traceability.mjs`, `construct intake done <id>`).** New `--output=<path>` flag stamps `intake_id`, `intake_confidence`, and `intake_rationale` into the artifact's YAML frontmatter when an intake packet produces an artifact. Refuses to overwrite a different `intake_id`. Companion `--no-output=<rejected|deferred|merged>` records why an intake was processed without producing an artifact. `docs:verify` walks `docs/prd/**`, `docs/research/**`, `.cx/knowledge/internal/**` and warns when an artifact lacks `intake_id` or an explicit `intake: none` declaration — surfaces drift without blocking CI.
- **Machine-enforced contract postconditions (`lib/contracts/validate.mjs#validatePostconditions`).** `agents/contracts.schema.json` extended to accept structured postconditions with three check kinds: `artifact-has-frontmatter-field`, `artifact-has-section`, `artifact-claims-cited` (every numeric claim has a `[source: …]` reference within 1 line of context). `validateHandoff` invokes these when the handoff envelope carries `artifactPath`. Backfilled on `researcher → product-manager` (requires Problem section, numeric claims cited) and `product-manager → architect` (requires Rejected Alternatives section, numeric claims cited).
- **Mermaid diagrams across the docs site.** Added flow or component diagrams to [`docs/concepts/deployment-model.md`](docs/concepts/deployment-model.md), [`intake-and-triage.md`](docs/concepts/intake-and-triage.md), [`gates-and-enforcement.md`](docs/concepts/gates-and-enforcement.md), [`learning-loops.md`](docs/concepts/learning-loops.md), the four `docs/start/` docs, and [`docs/cookbook/add-a-custom-agent.md`](docs/cookbook/add-a-custom-agent.md), [`fix-a-policy-violation.md`](docs/cookbook/fix-a-policy-violation.md), [`plug-in-your-own-llm.md`](docs/cookbook/plug-in-your-own-llm.md). Concepts now lead with a visual before the prose.
- **`docs/concepts/integrity-and-trust.mdx` — new public concept page.** Documents the four-layer integrity system: canonical no-fabrication rule, artifact-prose lint, intake traceability, and contract postconditions. Linked from the concepts index and from the architecture doc.
- **Cookbook pages cleaned up.** Removed stale HTML developer comments and redundant H1 headings from 11 cookbook pages that had accumulated them during earlier authoring. Frontmatter `title:` now drives the page heading, as Fumadocs expects.
- **`docs/cookbook/generate-artifacts.mdx` rewritten.** Covers the full workflow: template creation, intake provenance stamping with `--output=<path>`, `intake: none` opt-out declaration, and artifact-prose lint. Includes a visual flow diagram.
- **Custom visual component library for the docs site.** Replaced all Mermaid flowchart blocks — which were rendering as raw code — with four purpose-built React components (`FlowPipeline`, `SyncGrid`, `RequestFlow`, `DeployModes`) and Fumadocs' built-in `Steps`, `Cards`, `Tabs`, and `Accordions`. All 15+ pages in the `start/` and `concepts/` sections converted from `.md` to `.mdx` where needed. Home page install commands de-duplicated. Components live in `apps/docs/components/` and are globally registered in `mdx-components.tsx`.

### Changed

- **Memory MCP wiring switched from HTTP+SSE to stdio bridge.** cm (cass-memory) is the storage engine but doesn't implement the MCP HTTP+SSE handshake. OpenCode and Claude Code saw "memory SSE error: Non-200 status code (405)" because cm rejects GET on its endpoint and answers POST `initialize`/`ping` with "Unsupported method". `lib/mcp/memory-bridge.mjs` is now the protocol surface: it implements the MCP stdio transport natively (initialize, ping, notifications/initialized, shutdown) and forwards tools/list, tools/call, resources/list, resources/read to cm over its existing HTTP API. `construct sync` writes stdio config into editor MCP files in place of the broken remote URL, and rewrites any existing http/remote memory entries on the next sync. `construct doctor` now performs a real MCP handshake against every configured server via the new `mcp-protocol` watcher and reports protocol compatibility, not just port liveness. Backend URL configurable via `CONSTRUCT_MEMORY_BRIDGE_URL` (default `http://127.0.0.1:8765/`). Closes `construct-1qt`.
- **Removed all `geraldmaron.github.io/construct/v2/` links.** The docs site is published at `geraldmaron.github.io/construct/` with no versioned path. `README.md`, `construct_guide.md`, and `templates/docs/construct_guide.md` were updated.
- **`.cx/context.{md,json}` are no longer tracked.** They are pure per-machine session telemetry. Updated `.gitignore` and removed both files from the index via `git rm --cached`. Existing local files are untouched.

### Fixed

- **`pr-review.yml` workflow failed when `.cx/` did not exist in the CI checkout.** The fallback `echo` to `.cx/pr-review.json` requires the directory. Added `mkdir -p .cx` before the `construct review` call so the write always has a valid target, regardless of whether `.cx/` is tracked in the repo.
- **`lib/docs-verify.mjs` `.cx/context.md` gate restored to critical.** Existence and staleness are both critical when `.cx/` is present. CI passes because `.cx/` is gitignored and does not exist in fresh checkouts. `ensureCxDir` in `lib/project-init-shared.mjs` ensures every code path that creates `.cx/` also initializes `context.md`, so the invariant "`.cx/` present → `context.md` present" holds throughout the system.

- **Boundary registration is now an authenticated handshake.** `POST /api/embed/boundary/register` validates parent URL reachability and verifies an HMAC signature over `childInstanceId|nonce` when `CONSTRUCT_BOUNDARY_SECRET` is set. Conflicting parents are rejected unless `CONSTRUCT_BOUNDARY_ALLOW_OVERRIDE=1`; rotated configs are archived to `~/.construct/boundary.<ts>.json`. The bound state lives at `~/.construct/boundary.json` with `0600` permissions. Closes the multitenancy boundary stub.
- **Provider adapter extension contracts are now first-class.** `lib/cache-strategy-google.js` exposes `setCachedContentResolver(resolver)` so provider plugins can supply Gemini cachedContent resources without Construct owning the API call. `lib/providers/auth-manager.mjs` exposes `registerRefreshAdapter(provider, adapter)` for vendor-specific token rotation. `lib/provider-capabilities.js#probeProviderCapabilities` dispatches to an adapter's optional `probe(modelId)` export. Construct stays provider-agnostic; vendor integrations plug in through these contracts.
- **`construct reflect` auto-derives a summary when `--summary` is omitted.** Falls back to a digest of `.cx/context.md` plus the most recent session-summary observation. The auto-derived path is tagged `source:auto-derived` on the captured observation with a lower confidence (0.65 vs 0.9) so downstream consumers can distinguish operator-authored from agent-derived feedback.
- **Contract enforcement is now a real gate.** `agents/contracts.schema.json` defines the shape of `specialists/contracts.json`; `lib/contracts/validate.mjs` exposes `validateContractsFile` (sync-time + CI) and `validateHandoff` (runtime). `construct lint:contracts` is the new CLI command. `npm run lint:contracts` and a matching `lint-contracts` CI job validate every push that touches `agents/`. Sync (`scripts/sync-specialists.mjs`) fails fast when contracts diverge from the registry or reference schemas that no longer exist.
- **`workflow_contract_validate` MCP tool** routes a producer→consumer handoff through `validateHandoff`. Honors `CONSTRUCT_CONTRACT_ENFORCEMENT=warn|block` (default `warn`); `block` returns `BLOCKED_CONTRACT` with errors so the workflow refuses to advance.
- **Cross-surface consistency watcher** (`lib/doctor/watchers/consistency.mjs`). Five bundled drift checks run on a 15-minute cadence: hook manifest references resolve to real files; MCP tool exports match the dispatch table; `specialists/role-manifests.json` keys resolve to registry personas; persona `promptFile` references exist on disk; `specialists/contracts.json` validates (when the W2 validator is present). Blocking findings escalate; warnings record only. New `construct doctor consistency` CLI runs the checks once and exits non-zero on blocking findings.
- **Schema migration scaffolding** (`lib/migrations/`). `CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION` is the contract between the binary and machine-readable artifacts (registry, contracts, role-manifests, project config). New `construct migrate` CLI plans + applies migrations, defaults to dry-run preview with `--dry-run`. `checkCompatibility(artifactVersion)` surfaces three outcomes — compatible, needsMigration, needsUpgrade — so callers can route operators to the right action. v1 baseline stamps pre-versioned artifacts with `version: 1` without changing shape.
- **`lib/version.mjs`** centralizes installed-version reads and exposes `parseSemver` / `compareSemver` so doctor watchers and migration code stop duplicating `package.json` parsing.
- **Daemon safeguard contract** (`lib/daemons/contract.mjs`). `createDaemon(spec)` enforces bounded lifetime, idle shutdown, heartbeat, single-writer lock, killswitch env, and resource caps. `classifyPacket()` routes per-item TTL + retry-budget decisions. Every long-running Construct process is required to use this contract.
- **Intake daemon** (`lib/intake/daemon.mjs`). Polls `.cx/inbox/`, classifies new files, writes packets to `.cx/intake/pending/`, dead-letters TTL-exceeded or retry-exhausted items to `.cx/intake/dead-letter/`. Killswitch: `CONSTRUCT_INTAKE_DAEMON=off`.
- **Post-execution rule verifier hook** (`lib/hooks/rule-verifier.mjs`). Stop hook that audits the session transcript for consequential actions (commits, pushes, edits to protected files) and classifies whether each had a preceding approval signal. Intent-based — no specific word is required; the classifier is pluggable for LLM-graded inference. Records `.cx/audit.jsonl` entries with verdict pass / fail / inconclusive.
- **PR auto-review workflow** (`.github/workflows/pr-review.yml`). On `pull_request: [opened, synchronize, ready_for_review]` runs `construct review --persona=cx-reviewer` and posts findings as a single PR comment. Read-only — never commits, never auto-approves.
- **Release-gate functional test** (`tests/functional/release-gate.functional.test.mjs`) spawns the real `construct` binary against the repo and asserts every release-blocking gate exits cleanly: `--version`, `doctor`, `docs:verify`, `docs:update --check`, `lint:comments`, `lint:agents`, plus conditional checks for `lint:contracts` (W2), `doctor consistency` (W3), `migrate --dry-run` (W4), boundary contract (W1), daemon + rule-verifier surface (W5), and a final "no misleading wording" sweep across `lib/`, `bin/`, `scripts/`. Each conditional skip prints a clear reason so the gate self-activates as workstream PRs land. New `npm run release:gate` script.

### Changed

- **Comment lint blocks misleading "future implementation" wording.** New banned patterns: `phase [abc] follow-up`, `in a real implementation`, `would go here`, `coming soon`, `not yet supported`. Hits enforce on PostToolUse and via `construct lint:comments`. Existing offending comments rewritten honestly (no behavior change).

### Removed

- **Dead `getPendingActivations` export from `lib/hooks/proactive-activation.mjs`.** Had no consumers and carried a misleading "in a full implementation" comment.

### Fixed

- **Docs site build (Fumadocs) was breaking on unquoted colons in frontmatter values.** Quotes every affected `description:` and `title:` line so the YAML parses cleanly. Restores the Pages deploy.
- **Tailwind content scan was missing `./components/**/*.{ts,tsx}`.** All four custom diagram components (`FlowPipeline`, `SyncGrid`, `RequestFlow`, `DeployModes`) rendered their HTML but had no CSS — component-specific Tailwind classes (`border-emerald-500/30`, `bg-sky-500/10`, `min-w-[120px]`, etc.) were excluded from the generated bundle. Added `'./components/**/*.{ts,tsx}'` to `apps/docs/tailwind.config.ts` content paths. Components now render with full visual styling.
- **Duplicate H1 headings on three concept pages.** `prompt-surfaces.md`, `knowledge-layout.md`, and `embedding-boundary.md` each had a `# Heading` in the markdown body that duplicated the Fumadocs-generated title from frontmatter. Body H1s removed; opening paragraphs updated to introduce the page content directly.
- **`docs/concepts/embedding-boundary.md` rewrote from implementation spec to user docs.** Original content described API endpoints, JavaScript call patterns, test scripts, and environment variables — internal developer reference that was never useful to a doc site reader. Replaced with a user-facing explanation of what data stays local, what crosses the network, how modes work, and what Construct does not do.
- **`docs/concepts/prompt-surfaces.md` stripped of internal-spec sections.** Removed "Coverage policy" (fixture count requirements) and "Authoring guidance" (in-prompt example guidance) — both were developer-facing rules that do not belong in a public concept page. Simplified "Visual deliverables" section and removed Mermaid-as-preferred-tool reference.
- **`docs/concepts/learning-loops.mdx` added to the concepts navigation.** Page existed but was missing frontmatter and excluded from `meta.json`. Added title and description frontmatter; added to the concepts page list after `intake-and-triage`.
- **`AgentGrid` component** (`apps/docs/components/agent-grid.tsx`). Renders the full 28-specialist roster in a domain-grouped grid with color-coded accents (emerald R&D, sky Strategy, violet Engineering, amber Quality, rose Craft). Registered globally in `mdx-components.tsx`.
- **Fumadocs `Card`/`Cards`** registered in `mdx-components.tsx` for use across all MDX pages.
- **Diagrams added to 6 previously text-only pages.** `agents-and-personas.mdx` gets a dispatch FlowPipeline + full AgentGrid. `beads-and-state` converted to `.mdx` with a 5-store FlowPipeline. `prompt-surfaces` converted to `.mdx` with a routing FlowPipeline. `embedding-boundary` converted to `.mdx` with a local-vs-network FlowPipeline. `fix-a-policy-violation.mdx` gains a 3-layer RequestFlow (real-time hooks, commit/push, CI+PR). `use-the-inbox` converted to `.mdx` with an inbox-to-triage-packet FlowPipeline.

## [1.0.6] - 2026-05-23

### Added

- **MCP parity for the org profile and learning surfaces** (`lib/mcp/tools/profile.mjs`). The construct-mcp stdio server now registers 11 new tools wrapping the PR #67 capabilities so subagents reach them without shelling out: `profile_show`, `profile_list`, `profile_drafts`, `profile_health`, `outcomes_summary`, `outcomes_record`, `knowledge_add`, `profile_create`, `profile_archive`, `sandbox_list`, `learning_status`. Read-only tools are direct calls; mutating tools require `confirm=true` matching the existing storage_reset pattern; `profile_archive` additionally requires a substantive `reason`. `optimize_apply` and `optimize_rollback` stay CLI-only because they mutate registry state. Refs `construct-pxi`.
- **GraphRAG global query over the entity graph** (`lib/knowledge/graph.mjs`). New retrieval primitive answers "how does X relate across the project?" questions that pure semantic search handles poorly. Reads the existing entity store, builds an undirected graph from `relatedEntities[]`, detects communities, ranks them against the query via BM25 over central-member summaries, and returns structured communities with top members. Exposed to subagents as the `knowledge_graph_ask` MCP tool. Schema migration `db/schema/006_graph.sql` adds the Postgres projection for team and enterprise deployments; the JSONL entity store stays the source of truth in solo mode. Refs `construct-sl7`.
- **`construct profile set` previews structural changes before writing.** Shows a role / intake taxonomy / department diff between the current and target profile, supports `--dry-run` to preview without writing and `--yes` to skip the confirm prompt. Selecting the already-active profile is now a clean no-op. Refs `construct-78v`.
- **`construct profile archive` previews the destructive move before running.** Gathers a reason interactively when missing, lists the exact files that move and what stays (observations and outcomes are preserved), and confirms before any rename. Supports `--dry-run` and `--yes`. The 8-character minimum reason is enforced up front. Refs `construct-78v`.
- **Functional test for MCP parity** (`tests/functional/mcp-parity.functional.test.mjs`). Boots the real `lib/mcp/server.mjs` over stdio, performs the JSON-RPC handshake, and asserts each new surface is reachable and writes the expected durable artifact when confirmed. Closes the loop the unit tests can't see.

### Changed

- **Project documentation now follows `docs/STYLE.md` end-to-end.** A style pass landed across `docs/`, `specialists/prompts/`, `skills/`, `templates/`, `rules/`, `commands/`, `examples/`, and the root project docs. CHANGELOG history and `CLAUDE.md` remain exempt per the existing style rules.
- **`lint:prose` is now scope-correct.** Fenced code blocks and inline code spans are excluded from prose policy (code samples are not prose), and agent-platform sync targets (`.claude/`, `.codex/`, `.opencode/`) plus nested `node_modules/` are properly skipped.

### Removed

- **Two completed migration scripts**. Both ran once against the tree they targeted and the work has landed; the resulting test-suite references to them are also cleaned up.

### Fixed

- **mcp-manager test flake on Ubuntu node 22**. `makeRepoCopy` carried `.cx/` into the test fixture, and a stale `.cx/sync.lock` PID could collide with a live process on the runner. The `.cx` directory is now excluded from the copy so sync tests are self-contained.

## [1.0.5] - 2026-05-22

### Added

- **A1 session-end auto-reflect hook** (`lib/hooks/session-reflect.mjs`). Closes the manual-reflect gap. A Stop hook now runs at every session end, reads the transcript, and writes a deterministic `session-summary` observation into `.cx/observations/` via the existing `observation-store` API. The extractor (`lib/reflect/extractor.mjs`) is pure-function, O(n) over transcript lines, no LLM call. Hard 500ms wall-clock budget. Captures session id, project, and current git sha. Skips trivial sessions (no tool calls + very short final text). Opt out with `CONSTRUCT_REFLECT_AUTO=off`. `lib/reflect.mjs` exports `runReflectAuto({ transcriptPath, cwd, sessionId, durationMs })` for direct invocation. Registered in `platforms/claude/settings.template.json` as `stop:auto-reflect`. Refs `construct-h47`.
- **A2 research persistence** (`lib/knowledge/research-store.mjs`). `construct knowledge add --source=research --slug=<id> --topic="..." --confidence=<confirmed|inferred|weak>` ingests stdin into `.cx/knowledge/external/research/<slug>.md` with frontmatter (topic, confidence, sources, expiresAt, profile). Schema rejects `confidence=confirmed` without sources. Indexed for future hybrid search. The `commands/understand/research.md` prompt now ends with the persistence step. Refs `construct-32d`.
- **A3 specialist outcome capture** (`lib/outcomes/record.mjs`, `lib/outcomes/aggregate.mjs`). Every specialist outcome can be recorded into `.cx/outcomes/<role>.jsonl`. Aggregator produces `_summary.json` with per-role rolling success rate and 30-day window. `lib/intake/classify.mjs` now consumes `outcomeBoost(cwd, role)` as a tiebreaker capped at ±0.05 (regression-tested: clear keyword winners never get inverted). Refs `construct-bya`.
- **B1 profile schema and four principled profiles** (`schemas/profile.schema.json`, `profiles/{rnd,operations,creative,research}.json`, `lib/profiles/loader.mjs`). First-class org profile concept. Default is `rnd`, backward-compat with every existing project. Four profiles are organized by work loop (build / fulfill / produce / investigate) rather than industry. `construct.config.json` gets a `profile` field. Refs `construct-cuc`.
- **B2 profile-aware intake classifier** (`lib/intake/classify.mjs` refactor, `lib/intake/tables/{rnd,operations,creative,research}.mjs`). The classification table moved per-profile. RND behavior is locked byte-for-byte by `tests/intake/golden-rnd.test.mjs` written before the refactor. `classifyRdIntake({ profile, cwd })` accepts a profile id; default behavior unchanged for existing callers. Refs `construct-qto`.
- **B3 formalized flavor overlays + custom-profile escape hatch** (`schemas/flavor.schema.json`, `lib/flavors/loader.mjs`, `scripts/migrate-flavors.mjs`, `lib/profiles/validate-custom.mjs`, `scripts/lint-profiles.mjs`, `npm run lint:profiles`). All 53 overlays in `skills/roles/` migrated to declare `profiles: [rnd]` + `cap: 1`. Per-role flavor cap of 6 enforced. User-defined `.cx/profile.json` with `custom: true` is validated against the schema (max 8 stages, max 12 intake types, max 40 roles, classificationTable must live under `.cx/`). Refs `construct-heg`.
- **Learning telemetry dashboard** (`npm run learning:status`). One-screen table: active profile, observations + last-24h delta, research findings, per-role outcome rates. Single answer to "is this thing actually learning?" Refs `construct-mov`.
- **Functional testing discipline** (`tests/functional/`, `npm run test:functional`). Permanent home for end-to-end checks that exercise a feature in an isolated tmpdir against real code, no mocks. A1 and B1 ship with functional tests today; every multi-component change going forward must add one. See `tests/functional/README.md` and the new rule in `CLAUDE.md`.
- **Prose lint** (`scripts/lint-prose.mjs`, `npm run lint:prose`, `.proseignore`). No em-dashes in user-facing markdown. Default scope is changed-files-only so the rule prevents regressions without forcing a mass historical rewrite. `docs/STYLE.md` documents the voice and punctuation rules.
- **`docs/STYLE.md`** (new). Voice, punctuation, structure rules for everything in this repo. `CLAUDE.md` and `CONTRIBUTING.md` both reference it.
- **Open source side-project framing** in `README.md` and `CONTRIBUTING.md`. The README opens with a personal note ("I am not a developer, this is a side project"). Tone now consistent across high-traffic docs.
- **`construct profile` subcommand**. `show`, `list`, `set <id>` for switching the active org profile. `set` writes `construct.config.json` through the validated config writer.
- **A3 production trigger**: `lib/hooks/agent-tracker.mjs` now calls `recordOutcome` on every Task SubagentStop, so `.cx/outcomes/<role>.jsonl` fills in real use, not just synthetic test calls.
- **Profile-aware sync**: `scripts/sync-specialists.mjs` filters `registry.agents` by the active `profile.roles`. RND lists all specialists so behavior is unchanged for the default; non-RND profiles emit only their declared roles.
- **Profile-aware intake routing**: `lib/intake/prepare.mjs` resolves the active profile and passes both `profile` and `cwd` into `classifyRdIntake`, so production daemon classification follows the project's chosen loop and gets the A3 outcome boost.
- **`construct status` surfaces learning loops**: appended block with active profile, observation count + 24-hour delta, research file count, outcome-role count.
- **Pre-push gate runs `lint:prose` and `lint:profiles`**. CI workflows added: `lint-prose`, `lint-profiles`. The new rules now fail builds at push time and on every PR.
- **CI changes filter expanded** to include `profiles/**` and `schemas/**` under both `code` and `agents` paths so edits there actually trigger the relevant jobs.
- **Profile lifecycle as a research artifact, not a JSON exercise** (`docs/concepts/profile-lifecycle.md`, `lib/profiles/lifecycle.mjs`). New profiles go through discover (cx-ux-researcher), frame (cx-product-manager), architect (cx-architect), validate (cx-evaluator), promote, monitor, archive. `construct profile create <id> --role=<id> --department=<id>:<name>` scaffolds the draft profile, a requirements brief, one persona artifact per declared role (with the canonical Goals / Frustrations / Decision rights / Handoffs / Output contract / Failure modes / Evidence sections), and one charter doc per declared department. `construct profile drafts` lists in-progress drafts. `construct profile health <id>` reports per-profile observation counts and per-role outcome rates over a window. `construct profile archive <id> --reason="..."` retires a curated profile cleanly into `archive/profiles/<id>/`; observations and outcomes stay durable. `CLAUDE.md` requires the lifecycle for anything that lands in the curated catalog.
- **Persona and skill research methodology** (`docs/concepts/persona-research.md`, `templates/docs/persona-artifact.md`, `templates/docs/skill-artifact.md`). Grounded in Goodwin and Cooper for persona patterns, Galbraith STAR and Lawrence-Lorsch differentiation for departmental structure, and Bloom-style observable competencies for skills. Each persona declares decision rights, handoffs, output contract, failure modes, and evidence sources. Each skill declares scope, observable outcome, provenance, and the roles that use it. Without evidence the artifact is rejected at review.
- **Departmental structure on every profile**. The flat role list was misleading; real orgs delegate by department. Schema now requires `departments[]` with a non-trivial charter (>= 20 chars) per entry, and lint:profiles enforces that every declared role belongs to exactly one department in each profile. The four curated profiles (rnd, operations, creative, research) have been migrated to use departments grounded in their actual work loop.
- **Schema caps raised to reflect real org headcount.** Roles per profile: 40 → 80. Intake types: 12 → 24. Stages: 8 → 12. Department roster: 20 per department, 12 departments per profile. The earlier caps were arbitrary and would have forced bad shoehorning for any non-trivial org.
- **A4 evaluator-optimizer safety gate** (`scripts/optimize.mjs`). Inverted the default from auto-apply to preview-only. `--apply` writes a `.bak` next to the skill file before mutation and logs to `~/.cx/prompt-history/<agent>.jsonl`. Apply is rate-limited to once per agent per 7 days. `--rollback` restores from the latest backup. `lib/hooks/session-optimize.mjs` now passes `--dry-run` so hook-driven runs never modify prompts. New functional tests cover the gate.
- **Sandbox command** (`lib/sandbox.mjs`, `construct sandbox create|list|delete|prune`). Lightweight isolated tmpdirs under `~/.cx/sandboxes/<id>/` for specialist dry-runs and QA validation without polluting the working repo. Each sandbox gets a minimal `.cx/`, a stub `context.md`, and a `construct.config.json` (optionally with a profile id).
- **Outcomes tagged with active profile** in `lib/hooks/agent-tracker.mjs` so per-profile `construct profile health` rollups have real data, not just a project filter.
- **observation-store `extras` field**. Structured metadata travels in a first-class slot instead of being stuffed into `content` as appended JSON. Plain objects only, 2 KB cap. `lib/reflect.mjs` migrated; existing callers unaffected.
- **`construct init --profile=<id>`**. Persists the active profile into `construct.config.json` during first-time project setup. Validates against the curated catalog; unknown ids are rejected with the available list.
- **`construct drop`** is wired to the existing `lib/drop.mjs` implementation. The catalog has advertised it for a while; now it actually runs.
- **`construct docs <check|verify|update|site>`** parent dispatcher. The catalog had this listed but only the colon-namespaced forms (`docs:verify`, etc.) were callable. Both forms work now.
- **CLI catalog accuracy test** (`tests/cli-catalog-accuracy.test.mjs`). Every catalog entry must have a handler; every handler must be in the catalog or in an explicit allowlist. Caught the `docs` and `drop` gaps above.
- **Pre-release channel automation** in `.github/workflows/release.yml`. Semver pre-release tags (`v1.0.5-alpha.1`, `-beta.N`, `-rc.N`, or anything else with a `-` suffix routed to `next`) are detected and routed: npm publishes with `--tag <channel>` (so `latest` is not moved); Docker pushes `:<version>` and `:<channel>` but not `:latest`; Homebrew tap bump is skipped; the GitHub Release is marked pre-release. Pattern documented in `docs/maintenance/release-and-deploy.md` § Pre-release channels and referenced from `docs/maintenance/release-policy.md`.

### Fixed

- **Profile loader never read `construct.config.json`**: every caller passed only `cwd`, so the `profile` field set by `construct profile set` was silently ignored. `resolveActiveProfile` now reads the config file itself with precedence `explicit arg > .cx/profile.json (custom) > construct.config.json > rnd`. Locked by regression tests in `tests/profiles/loader.test.mjs`.

- **Doctor: copilot adapter parity false-drift**. `lib/parity.mjs` `checkCopilot` was the only surface that filtered out `internal: true` agents from the expected set while the sync script writes all 29 to `~/.github/prompts/`. The check now uses the shared `entriesForSurface` helper, matching Claude, OpenCode, and Codex. `construct doctor` is now 0 failed where it had been 2 failed pre-A1.
- **Doctor: git hooks unwired**. `core.hooksPath` set to `.beads/hooks` to activate the pre-commit policy gates.

### Fixed

- **Release publish: `setup-node@v6` + `registry-url` injects `github.token` as `NODE_AUTH_TOKEN`**, overriding npm's native OIDC Trusted Publishers exchange and causing a 404 on every tag push. Fix: remove `registry-url` from the `setup-node` step; configure the registry separately so npm can perform the OIDC exchange unobstructed. No `NPM_TOKEN` secret required or used.
- **Release publish: `npm whoami` gate added** before packaging begins — auth failures surface in seconds rather than after uploading 640 files.
- **Pre-release preflight script** (`scripts/pre-release-check.mjs`, `npm run release:preflight`): validates clean tree, on-main, CHANGELOG entry, tests, comment policy, docs:verify, npm audit, and `npm pack --dry-run` locally before tagging so CI is never the first to find a release problem.
- **Failed CI release runs purged**: 14 failed/cancelled release workflow runs deleted from Actions history.

## [1.0.4] - 2026-05-22

### Added

- **Dependabot** (`.github/dependabot.yml`): weekly npm and GitHub Actions version updates, grouped minor/patch and dev-tools PRs. Prevents action version drift.
- **CI: weekly secret scan**: `ci.yml` now runs gitleaks on a weekly schedule (Monday 08:00 UTC) in addition to every push — catches regressions in merged code.
- **CI: npm cache in test matrix**: `actions/setup-node` now uses `cache: npm` in the test matrix, matching all other jobs.
- **CI: engine-eval-retrieval coverage**: `tests/engine-eval-retrieval.test.mjs` added to the retrieval filter so changes to it trigger the evals job.
- **CI: skills/rules/templates in change filter**: changes to `skills/**`, `rules/**`, and `templates/**` now trigger the agents lint job (covers `tests/agent-prompts.test.mjs`).
- **Release gate hardened**: `node bin/construct doctor` and `node ./bin/construct docs:verify` added to `release.yml` gate for consistency with per-matrix CI checks.
- **Strategy store** (`lib/strategy-store.mjs`): dual-mode store (file + Postgres) for `.cx/strategy.md`. Exports `readStrategy`, `getStrategyDigest`, `writeStrategy`. Agents now ground synthesis decisions against declared Bets and Non-bets.
- **Strategy API**: `GET /api/strategy` and `PUT /api/strategy` dashboard endpoints backed by the new store.
- **Recommendations API**: `GET /api/recommendations`, `PATCH /api/recommendations` (dismiss/revive) — previously scored but invisible.
- **Postgres recommendations table** (`db/schema/004_recommendations.sql`): `construct_recommendations` with priority, dedup, suppression, and cross-project isolation. Recommendation store now writes to Postgres in team mode (best-effort, JSONL fallback preserved).
- **Postgres strategy table** (`db/schema/005_strategy.sql`): `construct_strategy` with version history per project.
- **Workspace type field**: `lib/embed/workspaces.mjs` now accepts `type` (`product`|`platform`|`enterprise`|`growth`|`ai-product`) on create/update; defaults to `'product'`.
- **Workspace-type overlay auto-selection**: `lib/prompt-composer.js` resolves role overlays from workspace type when caller doesn't provide explicit `roleFlavors`.
- **Strategy fragment in prompt pipeline**: strategy digest injected at priority-3 (same as context-digest) — agents see declared Bets/Non-bets without manual `get_skill()` call.
- **Product signal workflow rubrics**: `skills/docs/product-signal-workflow.md` fully rewritten with concrete confidence rubric (high/medium/low thresholds), contradiction resolution protocol, artifact decision tree with explicit conditions, and strategy alignment check.
- **Strategy workflow skill** (`skills/docs/strategy-workflow.md`): read, check signal alignment, and update the strategy store.
- **Strategy template** (`templates/docs/strategy.md`): canonical structure for Vision / Bets / Non-bets / Time Horizon / North Star / Competitive Positioning.
- **Multi-scope strategy store**: strategy is now stored per-scope under `.cx/knowledge/decisions/strategy/{scope}.md` (product, technical, gtm, platform) rather than a single flat file. `lib/strategy-store.mjs` exports `readStrategy(scope)`, `readAllStrategies()`, `listStrategyScopes()`, `getStrategyDigestSync()`. Strategy is never auto-updated from ingested documents — write always requires explicit user action.
- **Template conflict detection**: `lib/init-update.mjs` adds `checkTemplateConflicts(targetDir)` (SHA256 comparison of construct shipped templates vs project templates) and `resolveTemplateConflict(conflict, resolution)` with three resolution modes: keep-project, use-construct (with .bak backup), move-to-cx-override.
- **Auto-sync on git merge/checkout**: `.beads/hooks/post-merge` and `.beads/hooks/post-checkout` now run `construct sync --quiet` so platform files stay current after branch changes.
- **Agent roster disambiguation**: `skills/routing.md` now explains `cx-engineer` vs `cx-platform-engineer` distinction and when to use platform domain overlays vs. routing to cx-platform-engineer.
- **Strategy grounding in PM and architect prompts**: both `cx-product-manager.md` and `cx-architect.md` now reference strategy-workflow and require explicit conflict surfacing when a signal or decision contradicts a Non-bet.
- **Linux and Windows platform docs**: `docs/start/index.mdx` adds a platform support table and Linux/Windows install notes; `docs/start/connect-your-editor.mdx` adds Linux VS Code settings path.
- **cx-researcher rewrite**: persona now operates at principal-researcher/academic standard — recency-first discipline (most-recent-first), domain-specific authoritative starting points table, URL fetch-and-verify requirement, explicit scope boundary vs cx-ux-researcher and cx-rd-lead.
- **Research policy — recency-first discipline**: `rules/common/research.md` now mandates starting from the most recent year, defines domain-specific starting points by research type, and requires URL verification for all committed citations.
- **Research workflow — domain starting points**: `skills/docs/research-workflow.md` adds domain-specific starting point table and URL verification step.
- **Researcher role skill**: `skills/roles/researcher.md` adds anti-patterns for freshness blindness (most-recent-first), wrong starting point, and unverified URLs; self-check updated to match.
- **Research brief template**: `templates/docs/research-brief.md` rewritten with structured source table (class/date/URL/verified), per-finding observation/inference/confidence separation, counter-evidence section, and full reference format.
- **cx-docs-keeper cleanup**: removed non-functional Tool Contract stubs (create_document, update_context, record_decision with fake rate limits) and Parallel Execution section that misrepresented how role dispatch works.
- **Full persona alignment pass**: removed fake Tool Contract API stubs (with fictional rate limits) from cx-qa, cx-security, cx-sre, cx-ai-engineer, cx-platform-engineer, cx-data-analyst, and cx-orchestrator. Replaced fictional JavaScript code snippets in Parallel Execution sections with plain-text discipline descriptions. Fixed two cx-platform-engineer typos.
- **cx-orchestrator rewrite**: removed references to non-existent `specialists/contracts.json`, `orchestration_policy` MCP tool, and `agent_contract` MCP tool. Rewritten around real dispatch patterns with an explicit routing table, handoff format spec, and clear scope boundary vs cx-operations (runtime dispatch vs multi-session plan sequencing).
- **Research policy enforcement extended**: cx-ux-researcher, cx-explorer, and cx-rd-lead now explicitly reference `rules/common/research.md`. cx-ux-researcher clarifies primary vs secondary evidence for user behavioral findings.
- **Strategy grounding extended**: cx-business-strategist and cx-rd-lead now check `.cx/knowledge/decisions/strategy/` for declared Bets/Non-bets before drafting; cx-business-strategist EVIDENCE section requires dated primary sources.
- **Recency language fixed**: removed hardcoded year from all research policy language; replaced with "most-recent-first" so the rule stays valid across years.
- **Role manifest fence fixes**: business-strategist fence updated to `.cx/knowledge/decisions/strategy/**` (actual path); legal-compliance fence extended to include `docs/security/**` and `.cx/knowledge/**`; orchestrator fence corrected to reflect its read-heavy, dispatch-only role with appropriate allowedPaths.

### Fixed

- **CI: `aws-smoke.yml` migrated to OIDC**: removed `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID`/`AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY` static credentials; now uses `aws-actions/configure-aws-credentials@v4` with `secrets.AWS_DEPLOY_ROLE_ARN` and `id-token: write` — matches deploy.yml.
- **CI: Terraform version `1.7` → `~> 1.9`**: updated in `deploy.yml` (both `tf-plan` and `deploy` jobs) and `aws-smoke.yml`. Constraint allows 1.9+ within the 1.x series; Dependabot will keep it current.
- **CI: Terraform Plan hardened**: removed `continue-on-error: true`; plan step now differentiates exit code 2 (valid — changes detected) from real errors (exit 1 or other); "Post plan to PR" step runs with `if: always()` so plan output is always posted, including on failure; PR comment now shows ✅/❌ status.
- **CI: npm publish via OIDC Trusted Publishers**: `release.yml` publish job now uses npm's Trusted Publishers (OIDC) — no static `NPM_TOKEN` secret required. `id-token: write` permission provides the OIDC token; npm exchanges it for auth automatically. One-time setup: npmjs.com → package → Settings → Trusted Publishers → add GitHub Actions with repo + workflow. Provenance attestation included via `--provenance`.
- **CI: dead filter path removed**: `lib/evals/**` removed from retrieval filter in `ci.yml` — directory does not exist.
- **CI: `trivy-action` pinned off `@master`**: `aquasecurity/trivy-action@master` → `@0.28.0` in `release.yml` to eliminate supply-chain risk from floating master reference.
- **CI: `master` branch trigger removed**: `docs.yml` and `pages.yml` no longer trigger on `master` (repo uses `main` only — dead code).
- **CI: `docs.yml` bot push hardened**: added `git pull --rebase` before `git push` to prevent failures on concurrent pushes; simplified condition to exit early when no managed regions changed.
- **cx-orchestrator: `specialists/contracts.json` reference restored**: the persona rewrite incorrectly removed the reference to the real `specialists/contracts.json` contract file (which defines producer→consumer handoff types). Re-added as the authoritative routing substrate read before dispatch.

## [1.0.3] - 2026-05-20

### Fixed

- **Integration status icon**: Integrations found in any AI host config now show `✓` instead of `⚠`. The `⚠` icon is reserved for `degraded` services (those that failed a health probe), not for fully-configured integrations. `lib/status.mjs` `serviceIcon()`.
- **Memory integration always showed unavailable**: MCPs whose `hostSupport` has all entries set to `mode: 'unsupported'` (e.g. cass-memory, which is served through the built-in construct-mcp stdio server) now report `configured — Available via construct-mcp (built-in)` instead of `unavailable — Not configured in any host`. `lib/features.mjs` `buildFeatures()`.
- **Embed daemon crash on `emitTraceEvent(...).catch`**: `emitTraceEvent` is synchronous and returns a plain object, not a Promise. Three call sites in `lib/embed/daemon.mjs` called `.catch(() => {})` on the return value, throwing `TypeError: (intermediate value).catch is not a function` at runtime. Fixed by wrapping each call site in `try/catch`.
- **Telemetry setup log noise**: The `[embed] Telemetry setup skipped` message is no longer logged at daemon startup when telemetry credentials are not configured (solo mode default). Only unexpected errors are logged. `lib/embed/daemon.mjs`.
- **Telemetry and Memory (cm) no longer degrade overall health**: Both are optional services (`impactsOverall: false`). Missing telemetry credentials or a stopped cass-memory server no longer marks Construct as `degraded`. `lib/status.mjs`.
- **`construct init` silently skipped starting services**: `init-unified.mjs` imported `node:os` with destructuring (`const { os } = await import('node:os')`), but `os` is not a named export — the module itself is the default export. This caused `os.homedir()` to throw, the `catch` block swallowed the error, and `cm serve` (plus all other services) was never started. Fixed to `const { homedir } = await import('node:os')`. `lib/init-unified.mjs`.
- **Embed daemon start reliability**: `construct dev` (and `construct embed start`) now pass `rootDir` explicitly to avoid path-resolution fragility, close the log file descriptor in the parent process after spawn, verify the worker is still alive after a 400 ms liveness check, and surface any failure as a warning rather than silently swallowing it. `lib/embed/cli.mjs`, `bin/construct`.

### Added

- **`/api/intake/list` dashboard endpoint**: `GET /api/intake/list` returns the current intake queue (`{ items, total }`). Items are read via `createIntakeQueue(ROOT_DIR)` so it works in both filesystem (solo) and Postgres (team/enterprise) modes. `lib/server/index.mjs`.
- **Ingested knowledge in RAG corpus**: `buildCorpus()` now includes `.cx/knowledge/{internal,external,decisions,how-tos,reference}/` files as indexed chunks alongside ADRs, PRDs, RFCs, and snapshots. Files dropped into the inbox and processed by the embed daemon are immediately searchable via `construct knowledge ask`. `lib/knowledge/rag.mjs`.
- **`--version` / `-V` flags**: Standard CLI convention. `construct --version` and `construct -V` now print the version and exit, matching the behaviour of `construct version`. `bin/construct`.

## Unreleased

### Changed

- **Breaking: CLI command restructuring** — Complete overhaul of command structure for clarity and progressive disclosure.
  - **Renamed commands:** `setup`→`install`, `up`→`dev`, `down`→`stop`
  - **Removed commands:** `start`, `serve`, `show` (no backwards compatibility shims)
  - **Progressive disclosure:** Default `--help` shows only 11 core commands; `construct --all` shows all 60+
  - **Interactive mode:** Running `construct` with no args shows context-aware menu with smart suggestions
  - **Auto-magic health checks:** `construct dev` automatically checks prerequisites (Docker, cm, Node.js) before starting services
  - **Smart defaults:** `construct init` auto-starts services by default (use `--no-start` to opt out)
  - **Categories consolidated:** Core, Work, Integrations, Observability, Advanced
  - `lib/health-check.mjs` provides shared prerequisite checking across install/init/dev

- **Production-grade `construct init`** — Silent, non-interactive by default with smart defaults.
  - **Non-interactive default:** No prompts unless `--interactive` flag is used
  - **Silent git checks:** Working tree status only shown with `--verbose`
  - **Project type detection:** Auto-detects API, web app, CLI, platform, etc.
  - **Workflow-based docs:** Asks about specific needs (ADRs? RFCs? Runbooks?) not bland presets
  - **Granular flags:** `--with-adrs`, `--with-rfcs`, `--with-runbooks`, `--with-postmortems`, `--with-docs=adrs,rfcs`
  - **Smart suggestions:** Recommends docs based on detected project type
  - **Clean output:** Professional, emoji-free output suitable for CI
  - **New flags:** `--quiet` (minimal output), `--verbose` (detailed), `--interactive` (enable prompts)
  - **Auto-start:** Services start automatically without prompting (use `--no-start` to opt out)
  - Removed: `lean`, `product`, `full` presets (too generic)
  - Before: Noisy git status warnings, childish "lean/product/full" prompts
  - After: Silent initialization with project-aware documentation suggestions

### Added

- **Local-first telemetry adapter**: Added a shared telemetry client with `local`, `langfuse`, `http`, `otel`, and `none` backends. Local `.cx/traces/*.jsonl` capture is the default; Langfuse-compatible ingestion, generic HTTP ingestion, and OTLP-style export are opt-in via `CONSTRUCT_TRACE_BACKEND`. Legacy `remote` remains accepted and resolves from configured URL/key shape.

### Fixed

- **Telemetry service labeling**: `construct init`, `construct dev`, `construct status`, and onboarding docs no longer claim a Docker-managed local telemetry backend exists when only Postgres is managed locally. Status now reports local JSONL tracing or the configured remote exporter explicitly.
- **Managed Postgres port drift**: aligned the local service helper with the compose/setup default port `54329`.
- **Sandbox-brittle local state paths**: embed stores that previously captured `homedir()` at module load now resolve `.cx` paths through the shared path helpers, so runtime overrides and tests behave consistently. Hardened `recommendation-store`, `customer-profiles`, `workspaces`, and `conflict-detection`.
- **Sandbox-brittle contract tests**: sync/distribution and embed suites no longer depend on host temp-directory behavior that the sandbox remaps. `tests/sync-contract.test.mjs`, `tests/distribution-bootstrap.test.mjs`, `tests/embed/recommendation-store.test.mjs`, and `tests/embed/customer-profiles.test.mjs` now use explicit writable roots and overrideable `.cx` homes.
- **Release binary build**: Resolved four `esbuild` bundle errors that blocked all five platform binaries in the release workflow.
  - `bin/construct`: fixed incorrect dynamic import path `./lib/service-manager.mjs` → `../lib/service-manager.mjs` (relative to `bin/` location).
  - Created `lib/roles/catalog.mjs` (exports `listRoles`, `formatRoleList`) and `lib/roles/preference.mjs` (exports `getRolePreference`, `setRolePreference`) — both referenced by existing CLI commands but never committed.
  - `lib/evaluator-optimizer.mjs`: removed dead import of `./templates.mjs` (`getTemplate` was imported but never called; the file does not exist).
- **Docker builder stage**: Replaced `ENV CONSTRUCT_SKIP_POSTINSTALL=1` with `npm ci --ignore-scripts` so the postinstall hook is fully bypassed when only `package.json`/`package-lock.json` are present (before the `COPY . .` layer copies `bin/`).

## [1.0.2] - 2026-05-20

### Changed

- **Breaking: CLI command restructuring** — Complete overhaul of command structure for clarity and progressive disclosure.
  - **Renamed commands:** `setup`→`install`, `up`→`dev`, `down`→`stop`
  - **Removed commands:** `start`, `serve`, `show` (no backwards compatibility shims)
  - **Progressive disclosure:** Default `--help` shows only 11 core commands; `construct --all` shows all 60+
  - **Interactive mode:** Running `construct` with no args shows context-aware menu with smart suggestions
  - **Auto-magic health checks:** `construct dev` automatically checks prerequisites (Docker, cm, Node.js) before starting services
  - **Smart defaults:** `construct init` auto-starts services by default (use `--no-start` to opt out)
  - **Categories consolidated:** Core, Work, Integrations, Observability, Advanced
  - `lib/health-check.mjs` provides shared prerequisite checking across install/init/dev

- **Production-grade `construct init`** — Silent, non-interactive by default with smart defaults.
  - **Non-interactive default:** No prompts unless `--interactive` flag is used
  - **Silent git checks:** Working tree status only shown with `--verbose`
  - **Project type detection:** Auto-detects API, web app, CLI, platform, etc.
  - **Workflow-based docs:** Asks about specific needs (ADRs? RFCs? Runbooks?) not bland presets
  - **Granular flags:** `--with-adrs`, `--with-rfcs`, `--with-runbooks`, `--with-postmortems`, `--with-docs=adrs,rfcs`
  - **Smart suggestions:** Recommends docs based on detected project type
  - **Clean output:** Professional, emoji-free output suitable for CI
  - **New flags:** `--quiet` (minimal output), `--verbose` (detailed), `--interactive` (enable prompts)
  - **Auto-start:** Services start automatically without prompting (use `--no-start` to opt out)
  - Removed: `lean`, `product`, `full` presets (too generic)
  - Before: Noisy git status warnings, childish "lean/product/full" prompts
  - After: Silent initialization with project-aware documentation suggestions

### Added

- `construct upgrade` command — Upgrade to latest npm version with one command. Runs `npm install -g`, re-syncs all editor adapters, and verifies health. Usage: `construct upgrade [--yes]`. `lib/upgrade.mjs` implements the flow; `bin/construct` wires the handler; `lib/cli-commands.mjs` documents the command.
- Handoff contracts (`cx-handoff/v1`) and automatic lifecycle cleanup. `lib/handoffs/contract.mjs` defines a YAML frontmatter schema for handoff files — `schema`, `id`, `created`, `beads`, `status` (open/resolved/archived), `title` — plus required body sections ("What was done", "What's left"). `parseHandoff` is permissive (pre-contract files get `status: 'legacy'`); `validateHandoff` is strict (used when writing). `formatHandoff` produces contract-compliant markdown. `lib/handoffs/cleanup.mjs` implements three-phase lifecycle: resolved handoffs past retention → archived to `.cx/handoffs/archive/`; archived handoffs past 2× retention → deleted; open handoffs past max-items → warning only (open work is never auto-deleted). Wired into three callers: doctor daemon tick (30-min interval via `lib/doctor/watchers/handoffs.mjs`), `construct down` (best-effort at shutdown), and `construct handoffs prune` (manual). CLI expanded: `construct handoffs list` (all files with status/age/beads), `construct handoffs show <id>` (parsed frontmatter + sections), `construct handoffs validate` (strict check against contract), `construct handoffs prune` (run cleanup immediately).
